---

## 10. Templates

### 10.1 PR template (`pull_request_template.md`)
```markdown
## What & Why
<!-- one paragraph -->

## Tier
- [ ] TODAY  [ ] EXPERIMENTAL  [ ] FUTURE   <!-- required -->

## Scope
- [ ] New feature  [ ] Fix  [ ] Refactor  [ ] Docs  [ ] Security
- Touches: <!-- list modules -->

## Self-check (§4)
- [ ] Tier label + type hints + docstrings + logging present
- [ ] Ruff / Mypy clean
- [ ] Bandit no new findings
- [ ] Tests added & full suite green (921)
- [ ] No secrets; .gitignore updated if needed

## Security / Privacy note (if applicable)
<!-- capability/sandbox/IPC/crypto/subprocess/eval/self-healing/package-signing/installer/consent impact -->
```

### 10.2 Review comment format
```
🔴 **Security: eval() on untrusted input**
initrd/ai_helper.py:166 — `eval(line)` executes history-file content.

Why: a tampered or malicious history file yields arbitrary code execution,
directly violating the zero-trust model (design mandate); Bandit flags this B307.

Suggestion: parse the line with `ast.literal_eval` (if data-shaped) or a
small command parser; if execution is truly intended, require a capability
check + signature-verified history file.
```

---

## 11. Quick reference

- **Severity:** 🔴 must-fix · 🟡 fix-or-waive · 💭 discretionary
- **Tier labels (mandatory):** `[TODAY]` ✅ / `[EXPERIMENTAL]` 🔬 / `[FUTURE]` 🔮 — unlabelled = rejected
- **Per-file baseline (design mandate):** try/except + logging + type hints + Google docstrings
- **Command rule:** `execute(args=None) -> int`, `name/description/usage`, `--help`/`--version`, `_selftest()`
- **Zero-trust:** capability-gated, signed IPC, code-signed processes, PQC (Kyber/Dilithium, SHA3-512, AES-256-GCM)
- **AI self-healing:** sandbox + audit + rollback-tested; never auto-apply untrusted generated code to privileged paths
- **Privacy:** opt-in only; local-first; GDPR/erasure aligned
- **Tests:** `unittest`/`IsolatedAsyncioTestCase`; suite must stay ≥ 921 green; compressible data only in compression tests
- **CI to add:** Ruff, Mypy, Bandit (baselined, scoped), gitleaks, pip-audit, coverage gate, pre-commit
- **Review scope:** active Python only — exclude `Old Linux Code/`, `node_modules/`, `__pycache__/`, build artifacts
- **Resolved-in-direction inconsistencies:** H7 → **GPL-3.0 canonical** (fix Apache-2.0 strays, incl. `ai/providers.py` + `ai/resource_predictor.py` — H20); H11 → **Flutter (Dart) canonical** (decided 2026-08-20, overriding Kivy — drop `kivy` from `requirements.txt`, refresh Kivy refs as legacy, H15/§1.1); H14 → README is aspirational (don't review against it); H15 → pin deps, isolate `g4f`; **H16 → unify test runner + add CI test step**; **H17 → `SecureBoot.verify_image` must be fail-closed**
- **`ai/` study (v1.4):** backend = Python only, frontend = Flutter **(H11 conflict + "Flutter with java" is wrong → Dart)**; 🔴 H18 AI egress needs consent gate; 🟡 H19 consolidate duplicated `ai/` stacks + baseline; 🟡 H20 Apache-2.0 headers in `ai/`; 🟡→🔴 H21 self-healing patch must stay non-executable + gated
- **`ui/` study (v1.5):** frontend = **Flutter (Dart) canonical** in `ui/flutter_ui/` and **must follow HCI principles (§4.8)** — Nielsen's 10 heuristics + 2 UmerOS items; legacy `ui/*.py` (Python/Tkinter) is retired drift (H25); refresh `setup.py`/`README.md` Kivy refs (H22, extends H11/H15); dedupe `desktop_shell.dart` app registry into one `AppRegistry` (H23); rename jargon labels to plain language, e.g. "CPUIdle & Governor" → "Power & Performance" (H24); add `Semantics` + broaden Dart tests for a11y/WCAG 2.1 AA (H26). Frontend egress privacy reuses the backend consent gate (§4.3/H18)
- **`boot/` study (v1.6):** `boot/` is a `/boot` **management toolkit** (FHS 3.0 audit, GRUB2, systemd-boot/BLS, EFI+Secure Boot, UKI, bzImage, cmdline/sysctl, microcode, splash, kdump, initrd) for a **Linux-hosted** boot env — it is NOT the microkernel entry (`kernel/umer_kernel.py` is). High quality overall (type hints, docstrings, `_selftest()`), but: 🔴 H27 kernel `verify_kernel` fail-open (missing image ⇒ trusted), 🔴 H28 `is_binary_trusted` fail-open (DISABLED/SETUP_MODE ⇒ trusted, ignores `dbx`), 🟡→🔴 H29 waiver auto-accepted non-interactively, 🟡 H30 ten `boot/` files say Apache-2.0 (H7/H20), 🟡 H31 only `bootloader.py` has a tier label, 🟡 H32 `uefi_stub.c` is a non-functional C placeholder (no ctypes bridge), 🟡 H33 SHA3-256 vs SHA-256 vs mandated SHA3-512, 🟡 H34 `boot/` CLI skips the `core/command` contract + `demo_boot.py` uses unprefixed imports
- **`bin/` study (v1.7):** `bin/` = ~44 FHS `/bin` + `/usr/*` command modules (≈19k LOC) — the **user-facing shell command surface**. Subprocess usage is otherwise safe (arg-list + `timeout`, no `shell=True`, no `eval`/`os.system`/`pickle`/`yaml.load`), but it is the **dominant H6 offender**: 🟡 H35 **27/44** use wrong `def execute(self, *args)` (vs adopted `execute(args=None)->int`), 🟡 H36 `su` runs `[sh,"-c",command]` as another user + its `_exec_shell` stub silently `return 0`, 🔴 H37 `login -f`/`-F` sets `skip_auth=True` (auth bypass), 🟡 H38 `ChageCommand` defined twice (`usr_cmds.py:3045` + `usr_commands.py:1938`), 🟡 H39 broad `except` (H8), 🟡 H40 **0/44** carry a tier label. No Apache-2.0 headers (good — unlike `boot/`).
- **`build/` study (v1.8):** `build/` is **PyInstaller output** for the `UmerOS-GUI` freeze — of 16 files (~42 MB) only **2 are authored** (`UmerOS-GUI.spec` + empty `__init__.py`); the rest (`*.toc`/`PYZ-00.pyz`/`UmerOS-GUI.pkg`/`base_library.zip`/`localpycs/*.pyc`/`warn-*.txt`/`xref-*.html`) are **generated → out of review scope per §0**. The `.spec` is the only reviewable surface and reveals: 🟡 H41 build freezes `ui/umeros_gui.py` (the **retired Tkinter GUI**, H25) — ships the wrong frontend, not Flutter; 🟡→🔴 H42 `codesign_identity=None` (unsigned distributable — breaks zero-trust signed-artifact mandate) + `upx=True`; 🟡 H43 hardcoded absolute Windows path (non-portable); 🟡 H44 empty `datas`/`hiddenimports` + `optimize=0` + **no codified build** (extends H16); 💭 H45 spec lacks license/tier label. `warn-UmerOS-GUI.txt` is benign (standard Windows posix-module noise).
- **`cloud/` study (v1.9):** `cloud/` = the **OTA (over-the-air) update subsystem** — 3 files (`cloud/__init__.py` empty, `cloud/ota_updater/__init__.py` re-exports `UpdateManager`, `cloud/ota_updater/update_system.py` ~67 LOC). It is the **zero-trust update path** and the headline finding is a 🔴 **fail-open**: `verify_and_apply` (L48-60) returns `True` and applies the update even when `self.crypto is None` ("skipping signature check"), and when a crypto engine *is* present it only `self.crypto.sign(payload)` (self-signs) and prints "PASS (simulated)" — it never calls the real `CryptoEngine.verify` (`security/crypto_engine.py`/`quantum/crypto_pqc.py` exist but are unused). So **unsigned/unverified updates are accepted** — same class as H17/H27/H28, breaks the signed-`.umerpkg` mandate. Also: 🟡 H47 module skips the baseline (`print` not `logging`, no `from __future__`, no tier label — should be `[EXPERIMENTAL]` since it's simulated); 🟡 H48 hardcoded `update_url = "https://updates.umeros.dev/latest"` (no pinned source/key); 🟡 H49 docstrings overstate security ("Verify cryptographic signature") while faking it (no rollback/audit). No license headers (clean, like `bin/`).
- **`compatibility/` study (v1.10):** `compatibility/` = the **Universal Compatibility Layer** (4 files, ~645 LOC): `container_engine.py` (593 LOC — `ContainerEngine`/`ContainerInstance`/`WineShim`/`AndroidContainer`/`LinuxCompat`/`SyscallTranslator`), `container.py` (`ZeroTrustContainer` stub), `syscall_shim.py`, `__init__.py`. **Best tier-label discipline so far** — `container_engine.py` uses `[EXPERIMENTAL]`/TODAY/FUTURE throughout (good counterexample to `bin/` 0/44). Subprocess launches are **arg-list, no `shell=True`** (injection-safe). Findings: 🟡 H50 `container_engine.py:30` says `Licence: GPL-3.0 (GNU General Public License Version 3)` (H7/H30); 🔴 H51 `ZeroTrustContainer.execute_binary` (L12-23) calls `capabilities.check(...)` but only *prints* and still runs — a **fail-open zero-trust gate** (same class as H17/H27/H28/H46), and it's unused; 🟡 H52 foreign ELF/`.exe`/APK launched **unsandboxed** (no namespace/quotas, full env inherited) — must be sandboxed before `[TODAY]`; 🟡 H53 `container.py`+`syscall_shim.py` skip the baseline (`print`, no `from __future__`, no label); 💭 H54 two divergent container models (`ZeroTrustContainer` vs `ContainerEngine`).
- **`core/` study (v1.11):** `core/` = just 2 files — `core/command.py` (32 LOC, the **base `Command` class every `bin/` command inherits**) + `core/__init__.py` (1 line). It is the **root cause of the H6 contract drift**: `command.py:30` declares `execute(self, *args: Any) -> Any` (returns `Any`), so the 27/44 `bin/` modules using `def execute(self, *args)` are merely inheriting the wrong base signature (H35). Findings: 🟡 H55 fix the base class to `execute(args=None) -> int` to resolve H35 at the source; 🟡 H56 the base `Command` declares a `privileges: List[str]` field but **never enforces** it — zero-trust commands must gate execution on declared privileges via `CapabilityManager` (cf. H37/H4); 💭 H57 no tier label (should be `[TODAY]`) / no license header (clean, like `bin/`). Tiny, foundational, otherwise low-risk.
- **`dev/` study (v1.12):** `dev/` = ~43 FHS `/dev` **virtual device-node filesystem** modules (≈6,800 LOC): a central `DeviceManager`/`DeviceNode`/`DeviceType` (`dev/core.py`) registry plus ~40 pseudo-device / subsystem / command modules (null/zero/random/full/tty/ptmx, input, shm, net/tun, fuse, memory `/dev/mem`+`/dev/kmem`+`/dev/port`, SCSI, NVMe, KVM, dri, snd, and `udevadm`/`losetup`/`makedev`/`mknod` commands). **Cleanest folder security-wise so far**: no `eval`/`exec`/`os.system`/`subprocess`/`shell=True`/`pickle`/`yaml.load`/`ctypes` anywhere, and licensing is **clean GPLv3** (headers say `Licence: GPLv3` — zero Apache-2.0 strays, a good counterexample to `boot/`). But it repeats the tier-label gap: 💭 H58 **0/43** carry a tier label; 🟡 H59 the base `DeviceNode` defaults to `mode=0o666` (world-writable) and a long list of nodes are explicitly `0o666` (`null`/`zero`/`full`/`tty`/`ptmx`/`shm`/`tun`/`fuse`/`i2c`/misc-char/misc-system) — only the memory devices correctly use `0o640`; 🟡 H60 `DeviceManager.sync_to_filesystem()` (`dev/core.py:211`) materializes **real** device nodes via `os.mknod`/`os.mkfifo`/`os.symlink_to` with **no capability/privilege gate** (same "privileged op / no gate" family as H27/H28/H46/H51) — on a real `/dev` with host privileges this creates device special files incl. world-writable ones; 💭 H61 the `DeviceManager` singleton registry has no lock (low risk in single-threaded boot). `memory_devices.py` is the model to copy (`STRICT_DEVMEM` restricts `/dev/mem` to `ALLOWED_RANGES`, `/dev/kmem` `AVAILABLE=False`, access checks log warnings).
- **`drivers/` study (v1.13):** `drivers/` = **75 Python modules (~34,853 LOC)** — the **kernel driver subsystem** (bus/device/driver framework + ~70 subsystem drivers: irq, usb, crypto, pci, input, mtd, dmabuf, tty, i2c, gpio, cpuidle, device_io, clock, thermal, reset, spi, iio, pinctrl, console, edac, nvmem, regulator, nfc, mailbox, nvme, rtc, devfreq, pwm, dmaengine, scsi, ata, counter, acpi, virtio, soundwire, tee, phy, ntb, remoteproc, fpga, pwrseq, rpmsg, framebuffer, interconnect, led, extcon, rfkill, backlight, `pci_endpoint`, `example_*`/`hello_char_driver`). A large **simulation of the Linux driver model** (no real HW access). **Clean:** no `eval`/`exec`/`os.system`/`subprocess`/`shell=True`/`pickle`/`yaml.load` anywhere; `crypto.py` uses `os.urandom` (CSPRNG) for every nonce/key; `device_io.py` locks its registries, claims regions, tracks owner, protects read-only ports. **But mostly off-baseline + zero-trust-gapped:** 💭 H62 **0/75** tier labels (worst raw count); 🟡 H63 **0/75** GPL license headers (should be GPLv3 per H7; unlike `dev/`, none at all); 🔴 H64 `driver_service.py` falls back to a **hardcoded static JWT secret `"test-secret"`** when OIDC is unset and serves every `/proc` endpoint incl. `/pid/{pid}/environ` (process env/secrets) + an **unauthenticated `/metrics`** — weak-default/static-secret auth (same family as H17/H27/H28/H46/H51); 🟡 H66 the whole subsystem has **no capability gating** (MMIO/port/DMA `device_io.py`, PCI claim `pci.py`, crypto key-gen/RNG `crypto.py` callable by any code; only `driver_service.py` imports `security`, and only `tls_utils`); 💭 H65 `ctypes` imported but never called in `device_io.py`+`irq.py` (dead import / latent HW surface); 💭 H67 `crypto.py` registers weak algos (DES/MD5) + pure-Python non-constant-time ciphers; 💭 H68 `device_registry.py` + driver registries have no lock (`device_io.py` is the locked counterexample); 🟡 H69 **19/75** lack `from __future__` and `driver_service.py` uses `loguru` not `logging` (H8).

- **`etc/` study (v1.14):** `etc/` = 81 FHS `/etc` config-manager modules (~24.4k LOC); subprocess arg-list, `shell=True`=0 (injection-safe), and `critical_files.py`/`fstab_manager.py` follow secure perms/mount defaults (good). But: 💭 H70 **0/81** tier labels (largest raw count); 🟡 H71 license split — 28 GPLv3/GPL-3.0 ✓, **6 Apache-2.0 strays** (`__init__.py`, `config_manager.py`, `critical_files.py`, `network_config.py`, `passwd_group.py`, `shell_config.py`), **47/81 NO header at all** (incl. `pam_config.py` 1821, `fstab_manager.py`, `sudoers.py`) — majority unlicensed; 🟡 H72 `pam_config.py` weak-auth detector is **non-blocking** (flags `pam_permit`/`nullok` but still writes them — fail-open); 🟡 H73 `sudoers.py` + whole `etc/` write real privileged `/etc` paths with **no capability gate** (incl. `NOPASSWD:`). `issue_motd.py` host-shell-out already tracked (H5/§4.2), arg-list-safe.

- **`examples/` study (v1.15):** `examples/` = 2 tiny illustrative modules (62 LOC) — `run_demo.py` wires real `kernel.scheduler`/`kernel.ipc`/`kernel.hal`/`quantum.simulator`; `tasks.py` has 2 cooperative `asyncio` tasks. **Security-clean** (no subprocess/eval/exec/secret/network). But 💭 H74 **0/2** tier labels, **0/2** license headers, skips baseline (`print` not `logging`, no `from __future__`, no docstrings); 💭 H75 the demo touches privileged HAL/IPC **with no capability gate / no HMAC-signed IPC** — should model the zero-trust mandate since it's the first code contributors copy.

- **`feedback/` study (v1.16):** `feedback/` = 2 files (185 LOC) — a community-feedback subsystem **stub**. `models.py` is clean (from __future__, type hints, docstrings, uuid4, no dangerous calls). But 🟡 H76 the package is **BROKEN**: `__init__.py` imports 5 phantom modules (`collector`/`tracker`/`channels`/`gfdl`/`manager`) that don't exist → `import feedback` crashes (only `models.py` + `__init__.py` present; docstring over-promises a full framework); 🟡 H77 both files say `Licence: GPL-3.0 (GNU General Public License Version 3)` (H7 stray, +2); 💭 H78 **0/2** tier labels; 💭 H79 `FeedbackEntry` stores submitter PII with no consent/erasure hook (gate channels on §4.3/H18 when implemented).

- **`fs/` study (v1.17):** `fs/` = 3 files (777 LOC) — the **VFS layer over a Quantum Filesystem (QFS)** content-addressable store. **First folder in the study with a tier label present**: `qfs.py:2` docstring `[TODAY / EXPERIMENTAL]`. Security-clean (no subprocess/eval/exec/ctypes/shell=True/pickle), and `qfs.py` is a **model** for the rest of the repo: `logging.getLogger("UmerOS.QFS")` + `threading.Lock` on every shared store. But 🟡 H80 the VFS/QFS has **no capability gating** on any mutation (`write_file`/`delete_file`/`mkdir`/`snapshot`/`restore_snapshot` callable by any code, no `CapabilityManager` — zero-trust gap, same family as H27/H28/H46/H51/H60/H66/H73); 🟡 H81 license split — `qfs.py` says `Licence: GPL-3.0 (GNU General Public License Version 3)` (H7 stray), `vfs.py`/`__init__.py` have **no header**, and `vfs.py` uses `print` not `logging`; 💭 H82 `snapshot()` reaches into private `self._store._refs` + CAS addresses with **SHA3-256** while the design mandate says SHA3-512 (confirm/exempt/document).

- **`home/` study (v1.18):** `home/` = 10 files (1,553 LOC) — the **`/home` user-home subsystem** (FHS: `home_manager`/`dotfiles`/`user_profile`/`home_dirs`/`home_local`/`home_ssh`/`home_mail`/`home_quota`/`home_backup`). **Injection-safe at the Python level** (no subprocess/shell=True/eval/pickle/ctypes) — it is path-based, not shell-based. Good: `home_ssh` uses `secrets` CSPRNG + strict perms (0o700/0o600); `home_manager`/`home_dirs` build a correct FHS/XDG layout. But 🔴 H83 `home_backup.restore_backup` uses `tarfile.extractall` with **no `filter`** → tar traversal / arbitrary file write (CVE-2007-4559) + `rmtree`s the home first (data loss); 🟡 H84 **systemic path traversal via unvalidated `username`** (`self.home_path / username` used for mkdir/write/rmtree; 🔴 for destructive callers `remove_home`/`restore_backup`/`remove_user_dirs`/`remove_local`) + `message.id`/`name`/`subdir`; 🟡 H85 **no capability gating** on privileged `/home` mutations (create/remove home, SSH keys/authorized_keys, `/var/spool/mail`, backup/restore) — same family as H27/H28/H46/H51/H60/H66/H73/H80; 🟡 H86 `user_profile` interpolates unsanitized env/alias values into sourced `.profile`/`.bashrc` → shell injection; 🟡 H87 `home_ssh.generate_key` makes **fake** SSH keys + appends unsanitized `authorized_keys`; 💭 H88 0/10 tier labels / license headers / `from __future__` / `logging` (silent `pass` on chmod errors; unused `import os`; `rglob` disk scans).

- **`HostFiles/` study (v1.19):** `HostFiles/` = **3 plain-text asset files (~347 B)** — `welcome.txt` + `welcome_copy.txt` (identical `Welcome to Umer OS v2.0.0-Quantum`) + `chat_log.txt` (philosophy blurb, **no PII/secrets/code**). **Not Python → §4 baseline N/A**; recorded for completeness. 💭 H89 `welcome_copy.txt` is a **byte-for-byte duplicate** of `welcome.txt` (dead asset) + `chat_log.txt` is misnamed + verify version string; 🟡 H90 its only consumer `ui/umer_de.py` (`HostBridge`) writes VFS content to host disk via a weak basename (`..` escapes `HostFiles/`) + auto-opens it (`os.startfile`/`subprocess.call`) — host-integration exfil + execution sink, legacy (H25).

- **`initrd/` study (v1.20):** `initrd/` = **17 Python modules (5,112 LOC)** — the **early-boot initial-RAM-disk runtime+builder** . **Best baseline of any folder studied**: all 17 have `from __future__` + `logging` + docstrings + `_selftest()`; all 17 say `Licence: GPL-3.0 (GNU General Public License Version 3)` (H7). It runs as **PID 1 / uid 0** (pivot_root/mount/chroot/seteuid/exec) so it is the most security-critical subsystem. 🔴 H91 `ai_helper.py:166` `eval(line)` on a "trusted" boot history log re-locates **H2** into early boot (attacker-writable log → root code-exec, fail-open like H17/H27/H28/H46/H51/H64); 🔴 H92 **no capability gating** on the privileged boot ops (most sensitive occurrence of the H27/H28/H46/H51/H60/H66/H73/H80/H85 gap); 🔴 H93 **CPIO entry-name traversal** in `InitrdBuilder._unpack_to_dir` (`builder.py:342-360`) → arbitrary file write (cpio analogue of tar H83; in-memory VFS path is SAFE via `vfs_ops._split`); 🟡 H94 dynamic `__import__` (name not allow-listed); 🟡 H95 all 17 Apache-2.0 (single sweep → GPLv3); 💭 H96 **0/17** tier labels; 🟡 H97 SHA-256 vs mandated SHA3-512 (CSPRNG `os.urandom` is good). **Models to copy**: `vfs_ops._split` traversal-safe VFS; `module_resolver` fails CLOSED on unknown modules; opt-in `signals.install`.

- **`installer/` study (v1.21):** `installer/` = **3 files (~22 KB)** — the **deployment/install surface**: `installer.py` (real `UmerInstaller` wizard + `InstallLogger`), `install.py` (a *second*, non-functional `UmerInstaller` stub re-exported by `__init__.py`), `__init__.py`. **Injection-safe** (no subprocess/eval/exec/shell=True/pickle/ctypes). `installer.py` is a **baseline model** (from __future__, logging, docstrings, EULA fails-closed in non-interactive, OSError-guarded log flush, rollback-on-failure). But 🔴 H98 **two divergent `UmerInstaller` classes** — package re-exports the dead `install.py` stub (same as H38/H106); 🔴 H99 `install.py:display_waiver` **fails OPEN** (auto-accepts in `dry_run` default + non-TTY `return True`) — same family as H29/H17/H27/H28/H46/H51/H64; 🔴 H101 `rollback` `shutil.rmtree(install_root)` **unguarded + auto-triggered** (data-loss, no `/`/scope guard); 🟡 H100 `run(consent_override=True)` unguarded EULA bypass; 🟡 H102 no `CapabilityManager` gate on install ops (H27…H92 family); 🟡 H103 `copy_os_files` no `_safe_join` (copies dotfiles); 🟡 H104 `installer.py` Apache-2.0, others no header (H7); 💭 H105 `install.py` off-baseline; 🟡 H106 `install.py` dead stub ("Real installation would happen here"); 💭 H107 lazy stdlib imports; 🟡 H108 no source-integrity check; 🟡 H109 EULA over-promises backup+rollback
- **`kernel/` study (v1.22):** `kernel/` = 40 files (~10,131 LOC) — the **microkernel core** (`umer_kernel.py` 87 KB + `shell_commands.py` 82 KB). Strong baseline cluster (`capability_manager`/`ipc_bus`/`scheduler`/`panic`/`pid_allocator`/`signals`/`taint`/`sysctl`/`memory_manager`/`resource`/`softirq`/`reboot`/`audit`/`cred`/`workqueue`/`modules/dev` — all `from __future__`+logging+lock-disciplined). **Headline 🔴 H110:** live `UmerKernel` wires **no-op placeholders** for `MemoryManager`/`IPCBus`/`CapabilityManager` (real modules exist but commented out L1569-1571) → zero-trust gating + signed IPC + memory accounting all inert. 🔴 H111 dummy `CryptoEngine.verify` returns `True` (crypto fail-open); 🔴 H112 decorative `SecuritySandbox`; 🟡 H113 `sudo`/`su` no auth/cap check; 🟡 H114 privileged cmds un-gated; 🟡 H115 capability lifecycle unwired; 🟡 H116 GUI via `subprocess.Popen` of legacy Tkinter `ui/launch_gui.py`; 🟡 H117 `ipc_bus.try_receive` skips HMAC + no cap gate; 🟡 H118 `cred.is_root()` bypasses caps; 🟡 H119 cgroup implicit-allow; 🟡 H127 `panic()` `taint.add("TAINT_KERNEL_PANIC")` raises (not in `_FLAG_INDEX`). 💭 off-baseline toy cluster (H120-H126: `umer_kernel1` dep, triple header, Kivy drift, dead REPLs, import-time crypto, kivy req, udev `RUN` unimplemented + `0o666`, `0o666` dev nodes). the TODAY code doesn't do.
- **`legal/` study (v1.23):** legal/compliance/consent package (12 files, 92 KB). 🔴 **H7 license conflict lives here** — `licenses.py`+`README.md` codify Apache-2.0 as primary with no GPL-3.0 (H128); 🔴 license audit is fail-open (H129) + `get_license_text("GPL-3.0")` returns Apache-2.0 (H130); 🔴 consent gate fails OPEN via `require_consent_interactive` non-TTY auto-grant (H131) AND the `cli.py consent` subcommand hardcodes "I AGREE" (H135); 🟡 forgeable "cryptographic" consent token (H132), decorative DCO (H136) + hardcoded PQC/PGP fingerprints (H138), soft/fail-open `safety_check` (H139); 🟡/🔴 broken `test_legal.py` Test 4 (H140) + tests encode the fail-open consent CLI (H141); 💭 real PII/IBANs hardcoded (H137). Fix order: resolve H7 here → fail-closed consent → real DCO/fingerprints → fail-closed license audit → fix test.

---

- **`lib/` study (v1.24):** `lib/` = **33 source modules (25 top-level + 8 `lostfound/`)** — the **FHS `/lib` shared-library / ELF / dynamic-linker / filesystem-layout simulation** (PAM, SSL/TLS, kernel modules, iptables, kbd, oss, firmware, arch, multiarch, usr/lib, usr/include, var/lib, gconv/iconv, tmpfiles, ELF parser, ldd, lostfound/fsck). **Best-disciplined folder in the study**: every module has `from __future__` + `logging` + type hints + docstrings + `_selftest()`; **zero `subprocess`/`eval`/`exec`/`pickle`/`ctypes`/`shell=True`/`yaml.load` anywhere** (grep-verified); `lostfound/fsck.py` is correctly **fail-closed** (marks clean only when `superblock_ok and unresolved_orphans==0 and errors_found==errors_fixed`). 🔴 **H3 re-confirmed** — `security.py:72` `PASSWORD = "password"` hardcoded constant (latent); 🔴 **H146** `ssl_libs.py:414-427` `_check_is_trusted` returns `True` if ANY CA bundle file exists on disk + `check_trust` trusts any `is_ca` cert (trust/verify fail-open, H17/H111 family); 🔴 **H147** `ssl_libs.py:82-92` `is_expired` always `False` + `days_until_expiry` hardcoded `365` (certificates never expire); 💭 **H148** `ssl_libs.py` is a "simplified" string-parsed sim (needs `[EXPERIMENTAL]` tier); 🟡 **H149** ~23 `lib/` modules + `README.md:138` carry `Licence: GPL-3.0 (GNU General Public License Version 3)` — **largest Apache-2.0 cluster, contradicts H7 GPL-3.0 canonical**; 💭 **H150** `security.py` registers `pam_permit.so` "Always permit" as a stock PAM module (low-risk fail-open trap). Fix order: fail-closed `_check_is_trusted` + real expiry → resolve H7 (GPL-3.0) → remove H3 + H150 caveat.

- **`liboqs/` study (v1.25):** `liboqs/` = **upstream Open Quantum Safe C library, vendored UNPINNED** (no `.gitmodules` -> can silently drift; MIT license, conflicts with H7 GPL-3.0 canonical). 5,665 `.c`/`.h` files with **real, audited** FIPS-203/204/205 PQC (`ml_kem`=ML-KEM/Kyber, `ml_dsa`=ML-DSA/Dilithium, `slh_dsa`=SPHINCS+) - supplies the §4.2 PQC mandate **only if UmerOS binds to it**. Python surface = 28 upstream `scripts/`+`tests/` `.py` (injection-safe: `subprocess.run` list-args, no `shell=True`/`eval`/`exec`/`pickle`/`ctypes`; `return True` hits benign). **Live PQC is NOT wired to the vendored C** - UmerOS uses `quantum/crypto_pqc.py` (Apache-2.0 stray H7, **silent classical-crypto fallback** -> H152 🔴) + `kernel/pqcrypto_.py` (commented example, divergent backend -> H153 🟡). 🔴 **H154** `cloud/ota_updater/update_system.py` hardcoded fake PQC sig; 💭 **H155** `liboqs/` dead/unwired. **Fix order**: pin vendored lib -> relicense GPL-3.0 -> bind live PQC to C lib -> remove silent classical fallback -> wire OTA to real `CryptoEngine.verify`.
- **`media/` study (v1.26):** `media/` = **12 FHS `/media` removable-media modules** - well-disciplined baseline + injection-safe `subprocess` (arg-list, no `shell=True`) + safely-scoped cleanup, **but the privileged mount/auto-mount path is zero-trust-gapped and bypasses the hardening that exists**. 🔴 **H156** no `CapabilityManager` gate on `mount_ops.mount`/`auto_mount._handle_hotplug`/`udisks2.UDisks2Client.mount`; 🔴 **H157** `auto_mount._do_mount` mounts removable media `rw` without `noexec,nosuid,nodev` (bypasses `MountManager.allocate` + `mount_options_for`, setuid-on-USB -> priv-esc); 🟡 **H158** `permissions.py` authz unwired + `" optical"` literal bug; 🟡 **H159** `fstab.add()` skips `validate()` (detect-but-don't-fail-closed) + `make_removable_entry` omits hardening; 🟡 **H160** 5 Apache-2.0 strays + 7 missing headers (H7); 💭 **H161** 3 broken + 1 non-conformant `_selftest()` (`device_info`/`cleanup`/`hotplug` crash on `__main__`, `mount_manager` non-conformant); 💭 **H162** `cleanup.py __import__("re")` anti-pattern (cleanup scoping safe); 💭 **H163** `filesystem.FsType.NVME` nonexistent enum + `EXT4` dup; 💭 **H164** `mount_ops._real_unmount` dead code + lazy flag bug; 💭 **H165** `auto_mount.user_mode` decorative + auto-mount with no consent. **Models to copy**: `MountManager.allocate()` (L243) + `mount_options_for(removable=True)` (L407) already add `noexec,nosuid,nodev`; `fstab.FstabValidator` exists - enforce it. Fix order: capability-gate mount -> wire auto_mount/udisks2 through `MountManager`/`mount_options_for` -> wire `permissions.py` -> enforce `fstab.validate()` -> fix 4 `_selftest()`s + `FsType.NVME` + `" optical"` + dead `_real_unmount` -> resolve H7.
- **`mnt/` study (v1.27):** `mnt/` = **7 FHS `/mnt` admin-mount modules** (the privileged counterpart to `/media`) - full baseline + **all 7 `_selftest()`s FUNCTIONAL** (better than media) + injection-safe; `audit.py` is a model (key-filtered JSONL deserialization). 🔴 **H166** no `CapabilityManager` gate on `MountManager`/`MountPointManager`/`Fstab.write_file`; 🔴 **H167** `MountPointManager.remove(force=True)` `shutil.rmtree` on a non-symlink-checked path (TOCTOU arbitrary delete); 🔴 **H168** `Fstab.write_file` un-gated `/etc/fstab` write + drops comments; 🟡 **H169** user mounts add `nosuid,nodev` but not `noexec` (`MountManager` never auto-hardens `/mnt`); 🟡 **H170** `MntValidator` advisory-only (detect-don't-fail-closed); 🟡 **H171** `_save_mtab` rewrites whole `/etc/mtab` from partial list; 💭 **H172** `_enforce_user` dead flag; 💭 **H173** `int()` on fstab `dump`/`pass` uncaught; 💭 **H174** `to_string` drops comments; 💭 **H175** shell-string logging pattern; 🟡 **H176** 6 Apache-2.0 strays + missing `__init__` header (H7). **Fix order**: capability-gate mount/remove/fstab-write -> symlink/realpath guard on `remove(force=True)` -> `noexec,nosuid,nodev` for `/mnt` user mounts -> enforce `MntValidator` -> fix mtab clobber -> resolve H7.
- **`network/` study (v1.28):** `network/` = **6 networking modules** - well-disciplined baseline + injection-safe `subprocess` (argv-list, no `shell=True`) + TLS verified by default + `tcp_server.py` is a **model for abuse controls** (conn caps, per-IP rate limit, read timeout, max msg size). **But the whole egress surface is zero-trust-gapped and one "encryption" is a footgun**. 🔴 **H177** no `CapabilityManager` gate on ANY egress (DNS/HTTP/TCP/VPN); 🔴 **H178** `HTTPClient._validate_url` doesn't block loopback/link-local/private -> SSRF (`http://169.254.169.254` metadata passes); 🟡 **H179** `VPNTunnel._xor_frame` is reversible XOR, not encryption (honestly "simulation" but a footgun); 🟡 **H180** `DNSResolver` uses plaintext host resolver, DoH never enforced (spoofable); 💭 **H181** no license headers + `__init__` missing `from __future__` (H7); 💭 **H182** no `_selftest()` in any module. **Models to copy**: `tcp_server.py` abuse controls, `VPNClient` argv-list subprocess, `VPNTunnel` transparent "simulation" labeling. **Fix order**: capability-gate egress -> SSRF egress policy (resolve + block internal ranges) -> replace XOR with AEAD/refuse-no-engine -> DoH/DNSSEC default -> GPL headers + future import -> `_selftest()`s.

- **`opt/` study (v1.29):** `opt/` = **14 `/opt` add-on-software modules** - better baseline than media/mnt/network (env/fhs/hierarchy/var have `from __future__` + `logging` + functional `_selftest()`; `fhs.OptFHSValidator` is a model auditor). **But the privileged install/remove/write surface is zero-trust-gapped with concrete bugs**: 🔴 **H184** no `CapabilityManager` gate on any privileged op (installs `$PATH` entries); 🔴 **H185** unvalidated `filename`/`config_file` → arbitrary file read/write outside `/var/opt` & `/etc/opt`; 🔴 **H186** unvalidated `name`/`provider` in `shutil.rmtree` → arbitrary dir deletion; 🔴 **H187** unescaped `command`/`args` in generated launcher/wrapper scripts → code execution. 🟡 H188 discovered `bin_path` injected into `/etc/profile.d` + `~/.bashrc`; 🟡 H189 `OptPackage` hardcodes real `/etc/opt`/`/var/opt`; 🟡 H193 service manager executes any discovered script. 💭 H190 `verify_integrity` mislabeled (no hashing); H191 missing `from __future__` in 10 modules; H192 no `_selftest()` in 10 modules (`test_opt.py` ignores them + no security tests). **Fix order**: capability-gate all privileged ops → validate `name`/`provider`/`filename`/`config_file` (reject `..`, `realpath` under root) → `shlex.quote` launcher & profile snippets → make `OptPackage` honor `opt_root` → real integrity (hash/sign) → resolve H7 + `from __future__` + `_selftest()`s.
- **`packages/` study (v1.30):** 3 modules (635 LOC) — user-space package manager (`UmerPackageManager`). 🔴 H194 tar-slip on `extractall` (no `filter=` + naive `files/` prefix); 🔴 H195 untrusted manifest `name`/`version` path traversal; 🔴 H196 "Signed" overstated + fail-open when HASH absent; 🔴 H197 integrity skips `files/` payload; 🔴 H198 no cap gate on install/remove/update. 🟡 H199 lexicographic version compare; 🟡 H200 H7 Apache-2.0 stray; 🟡 H201 fuzzy registry match; 🟡 H204 symlink extraction. 💭 H202 missing `from __future__`; 💭 H203 no `_selftest()`.
- **`proc/` study (v1.31):** 24 modules (3,203 LOC) — FHS `/proc` virtual FS. **Zero dangerous calls** (cleanest folder) but the **writable surface is fully zero-trust-gapped** (same family as H156/H166/H177/H184/H198). 🔴 H205 `procfs.write`/`ProcFile.write` enforce only per-file mode, no cap gate; 🔴 H206 ~60 `/proc/sys/*` sysctls mutated via `setattr`/`registry.set` ungated (posture/DoS); 🔴 H207 `oom_score_adj` writable ungated; 🔴 H208 `/proc/irq/<n>/smp_affinity` writable ungated. 🟡 H209 silent no-op rw writes; 🟡 H210 hostname/domainname bypass registry + persist trailing newline; 🟡 H211 fabricated `CapEff`/hardcoded `environ` (label simulated). 💭 H212 strong baseline; 💭 H213 missing GPL headers; 💭 H214 simulated fallback. **Fix order**: capability-gate `ProcFileSystem.write` + sinks (`sys_admin`/net→`cap_net_admin`/oom→`cap_sys_admin`/irq→`cap_sys_nice`) → owner/UID check → fail-closed no-op writes → strip trailing newline → label simulated → add GPL headers.
- **`quantum/` study (v1.32):** 62 modules (~24,641 LOC) — quantum SDK/simulator. **Zero dangerous calls** (cleanest alongside proc/) but the surface is **crypto/secret/credential-centric**: 4 🔴 + 3 🟡 + 4 💭. 🔴 H215 H7 Apache-2.0 stray (`crypto_pqc.py:22`); 🔴 H216 `PostQuantumCrypto` silent classical downgrade (H152 re-confirmed); 🔴 H217 `AuthManager.save_to_file` plaintext creds (no 0600/keyring); 🔴 H221 `quantum_server` binds `0.0.0.0:8420` + wildcard CORS + no auth. 🟡 H218 no TLS cert pinning on provider auth; 🟡 H219 `qkd.key_reconciliation` toy (defeats QKD); 🟡 H220 `qrng` is a simulator, not true entropy. 💭 H222 CLI `--token` plaintext argv; 💭 H223 2nd Apache-2.0 stray (`quantum_sim.py:20`); 💭 H224 `IBMBackend` `print` may leak token; 💭 H225 provider REST tokens in clear (overlaps H218). **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7) → fail-closed PQC (no silent classical) → encrypt/0600 cred store → authn + localhost + CORS allowlist on `quantum_server` → TLS pinning → fix `key_reconciliation` + relabel `qrng`.

- **`root/` study (v1.33):** 9 modules (~87 KB) — FHS `/root` admin-home manager. **Zero dangerous calls** (cleanest alongside proc/quantum) + safe `os.chmod` everywhere + hardened `shell.py` (strips `LD_*` vars, `shlex.quote`s exports) + model read-only `safety.py`. 3 🟡 + 3 💭. 🟡 H226 10 Apache-2.0 strays (largest single-folder cluster, H7); 🟡 H227 `passwd.write()`/`upsert()` rewrite `/etc/passwd` ungated (cap family); 🟡 H228 `mail.ensure()` creates home @umask (~0755) not 0700. 💭 H229 `.bak` world-readable; 💭 H230 `home.ensure` chmod follows symlink + arbitrary CLI path; 💭 H231 dotfile template name traversal. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7) → capability-gate the `/etc/passwd` write → tighten mail home to 0700 → 0600 `.bak` → symlink/realpath guard on `ensure` → validate template names.
- **`sbin/` study (v1.34):** 9 modules (~95 KB) — FHS `/sbin` privileged-command model/registry, **fully simulated** (zero dangerous calls, zero real syscalls). Cleanest folder for both hygiene AND posture. 1 🟡 + 3 💭. 🟡 H232 missing GPL-3.0 header in all 9 modules (H7 missing-header variant). 💭 H233 `SbinManager.execute()` no cap gate / no audit log; 💭 H234 mount/filesystem ops simulated, real wiring must harden (`nosuid,nodev,noexec` + path checks); 💭 H235 commands masquerade as functional (false state), only `mktemp` does a real safe write. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7) → when real syscalls wired, capability-gate + audit-log → mount hardening + path validation → label simulated.
- **`scripts/` study (v1.35):** 2 tooling scripts (1,736 B) — dev/CI helpers, NOT a shipped runtime. Bright spot: both use `subprocess` injection-safe (argv-list, no `shell=True`). 2 🟡 + 2 💭. 🟡 H236 missing GPL-3.0 header (both files, H7 missing-header); 🟡 H237 `test_endpoint.generate_test_jwt` hardcodes `secret="test-secret"` (HS256) = same as `drivers.driver_service` 🔴 H64, test legitimates broken auth. 💭 H238 fixed `sleep(3)` readiness + forged-token bypass; 💭 H239 `pip install -r requirements.txt` unpinned (supply-chain) + buffered output. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7) → rotate prod secret + ephemeral test key (H64/H237) → poll readiness not `sleep(3)` → pin `requirements.txt` hashes.
- **`sdk/` study (v1.36):** 3 modules (~13 KB) — developer SDK (`UmerApp` base class + `BuildTool` scaffolder + example). Clean hygiene + good zero-trust delegation pattern (all FS/IPC/AI via bound `kernel_api` with `hasattr`+`RuntimeError` guard, never direct FS). 2 🟡 + 2 💭. 🟡 H240 missing GPL-3.0 header (all 3, H7 missing-header); 🟡 H241 unvalidated `app_name` → VFS path traversal (`/sdk/projects/{name}`). 💭 H242 `custom_app_code` written verbatim (untrusted templating); 💭 H243 no `from __future__`/`__all__`/`_selftest`. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7) → validate `app_name` → sanitize `custom_app_code` → add `__all__` + `_selftest()`.
- **`security/` study (v1.37):** 14 modules (~127 KB) -- OS security subsystem (6 top-level + `antivirus/` 8-module subpackage). Mixed: real crypto/TLS/hashing/AV-mechanics BUT security-specific false claims. 3 🔴 + 8 🟡 + 4 💭. 🔴 H244 SecureBoot fail-open default (allow-unknown); 🔴 H245 unauthenticated AV API (destructive endpoints, localhost); 🔴 H246 SecuritySandbox inert/masquerading (no real isolation). 🟡 H247 IPC anti-replay claimed-not-implemented; 🟡 H248 AIBehavioralMonitor decorative+cosmetic quarantine; 🟡 H249 verify_signature mislabeled (hash not key); 🟡 H250 AIFirewall toy masquerading; 🟡 H251 quarantine restore path-traversal/arbitrary write + deletes original; 🟡 H252 heuristics memory-DoS + Windows-centric; 🟡 H253 signatures fake hashes + silent JSON swallow; 🟡 H254 missing GPL header (all 14, H7). 💭 H255 realtime polling not FS-events; 💭 H256 dead code (~900 lines) + no `_selftest`; 💭 H257 tls_utils doc mismatch (TLS1.3-first vs min1.2) + 0.0.0.0 default; 💭 H258 crypto_engine ephemeral key + print. **Fix order**: SecureBoot strict-default -> authn+cap-gate AV API -> wire SecuritySandbox to CapabilityManager -> IPC replay -> real/label AI+firewall -> validate quarantine restore + do not delete -> cap heuristic size -> real/label signatures -> GPL headers (H7).
- **`sources/` study (v1.38):** 9 modules (~65 KB / 1,724 LOC) -- documentation/reference subsystem (bibliography, System V signals, glossary, kernel-doc specs, `/usr/src` skeleton manager, CLI). **Zero dangerous calls** (only `os.walk`/`open` reads). 0 🔴 + 4 🟡 + 2 💭. 🟡 H259 Apache-2.0 strays (8 modules, H7); 🟡 H260 `test_sources.py` missing GPL header + `from __future__` (H7); 🟡 H261 fragile absolute imports + sys.path hack; 🟡 H262 hardcoded Windows src-root + `__init__`-time mkdir side effect. 💭 H263 unused `shutil`; 💭 H264 signals "inter-process" doc overclaim. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7) -> relative imports + drop sys.path hacks -> runtime-resolve src root + move mkdir out of `__init__` -> drop dead import -> soften docs.
- **`srv/` study (v1.39):** 10 modules (~2,352 LOC) -- FHS `/srv` site-specific service-data hierarchy manager (per-service trees under `/srv/<service>`, POSIX perm profiles, www/ftp/git/rsync/tftp/samba/nfs protocol handlers, tar/zip backup+restore, registry management, `srv_ctl` CLI). **One of the few folders with LIVE dangerous FS calls**: `tar.extractall`/`zip.extractall` with no `filter=` + `shutil.rmtree`×3 destructive. 4 🔴 + 5 🟡 + 4 💭. 🔴 H265 tar extract no `filter=` (CVE-2007-4559 path traversal); 🔴 H266 zip extract no `filter=` (zip-slip); 🔴 H267 `restore_backup` `rmtree(dest_folder)` no cap gate; 🔴 H268 `delete_service_tree` `rmtree(target)` gated only by `force` (no cap). 🟡 H269 9 Apache-2.0 strays (H7); 🟡 H270 hardcoded Windows `/srv`/`.backups`/registry paths + `__init__`-time mkdir; 🟡 H271 fragile absolute imports + sys.path hack; 🟡 H272 NFS/Samba config-injection (unvalidated interpolate); 🟡 H273 `apply_profile` `os.chmod` no cap gate. 💭 H274 localhost test HTTP server no auth; 💭 H275 `test_srv.py` missing GPL header + `from __future__`; 💭 H276 unused `import json`; 💭 H277 unused `Tuple`. **Fix order**: add `filter="data"` to extractall (H265/H266) -> capability-gate rmtree (H267/H268) -> GPL headers (H7 H269,H275) -> decouple paths from __init__ + runtime/arg resolve (H270) -> relative imports (H271) -> validate/escape config inputs (H272) -> cap-gate chmod when real-wired (H273) -> drop dead imports (H276/H277) -> localhost+auth test server (H274).
- **`tmp/` study (v1.40):** 11 modules (~65 KB) -- FHS `/tmp` transient-files manager (race-free secure temp creation via `secrets`+`O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW` 0600/0700, process locks, tmpwatch/systemd-tmpfiles reaper, in-memory TmpFS w/ quotas, `tmp_ctl` CLI, socket-dir provisioning). **Best-engineered folder for secure-temp discipline** (reaper excludes protected sockets; tmpfs enforces quota; secure_io is a model). **0 🔴 + 6 🟡 + 4 💭** (no new 🔴). 🟡 H278 10 Apache-2.0 strays (H7); 🟡 H279 hardcoded Windows `DEFAULT_TMP_ROOT` + `__init__`-time mkdir (centralized in `fhs`); 🟡 H280 fragile absolute imports + `sys.path.insert`; 🟡 H281 reaper deletes by age/quota, no cap gate + no `tmp_root` containment; 🟡 H282 `tmpfs.sync_to_disk` unvalidated node name -> path traversal/arbitrary write (CWE-22); 🟡 H283 `enforce_permissions` `os.chmod 1777` no cap gate (correct mode). 💭 H284 `test_tmp.py` missing GPL header + `from __future__`; 💭 H285 dead imports (`shutil`/`json`/`Tuple`); 💭 H286 lazy `ctypes` Win32 PID check; 💭 H287 global singleton bootstrap hidden init side effect. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7 H278,H284) -> runtime/arg resolve tmp root + explicit bootstrap (H279/H287) -> relative imports (H280) -> cap-gate reaper + assert `tmp_root` (H281) -> validate tmpfs names (H282) -> cap-gate chmod (H283) -> drop dead imports (H285) -> document ctypes shim (H286).
- **`tools/` study (v1.41):** 1 module (`installer.py`, ~967 B) -- standalone OS installer script (NOT a package). Clean dangerous-call surface (ZERO subprocess/eval/exec/tarfile/zipfile/os.chmod/sys.path/Windows paths) but installer posture is zero-trust-gapped. 0 🔴 + 5 🟡 + 3 💭. 🟡 H288 `rmtree(dst)` no cap gate (bounded to allowlisted subdirs, 🟡 not 🔴); 🟡 H289 hardcoded `target_dir="/umer_os"`; 🟡 H290 CWD-relative `src` resolution (fail-open); 🟡 H291 missing GPL header + `from __future__` (H7); 🟡 H292 interactive `input()` EULA, no GPL alignment. 💭 H293 `copytree` fails open (silent partial install); 💭 H294 `logging` unconfigured (dropped records); 💭 H295 no docstring/`__all__`/type hints. **Fix order**: GPL header (H7 H291) -> cap-gate rmtree (H288) -> runtime/arg `target_dir` (H289) -> resolve `src` from script dir (H290) -> surface missing sources (H293) -> configure logging (H294) -> non-interactive EULA (H292/H295).
- **`usr/` study (v1.42):** 61 modules (~22.9k LOC) -- FHS `/usr` hierarchy manager, **MIXED**: ~20 LIVE (real FS writes at system paths) + ~41 SIMULATED. Clean dangerous surface (ZERO subprocess/eval/exec/tarfile/zipfile/ctypes; only scoped `shutil.rmtree` in 3 modules). **0 🔴 + 5 🟡 + 2 💭**. 🟡 H296 privileged FS ops no cap gate (sendmail/misc_data/bsd_compat/games_data/xml); 🟡 H297 construction-time `mkdir` at `/usr/share/...` (import side effect); 🟡 H298 fail-open `except Exception: return False`; 🟡 H299 H7 missing GPL header -- only 3/61 licensed, **58/61 unlicensed** (largest cluster); 🟡 H300 hardcoded `/usr/...` system paths. 💭 H301 SIMULATED privileged/kernel-surface modules masquerade (lib/sbin/rpm/gcc/x11 + io_uring/landlock/seccomp/ntsync/iommufd/dmabuf/vduse/tee/netlink/procfs/sysfs/syscalls, no real syscall wiring); 💭 H302 baseline gaps + `usr/` ships no tests. **Fix order**: GPL headers (H7 H299) -> cap-gate LIVE ops (H296) + move `mkdir` out of `__init__` (H297) -> runtime/arg-resolve `/usr` roots (H300) -> surface fail-open `except` (H298) -> label/wire simulated + kernel-surface (H301) -> baseline + `test_usr.py` (H302).

- **`var/` study (v1.43):** 4 modules (~706 LOC) + 2 data files — FHS `/var` manager, **100% LIVE** (no SIMULATED). 1 🔴 + 3 🟡 + 1 💭: 🔴 H303 path-traversal → arbitrary rmtree/mkdir/write/unlink + **cron RCE** (unsanitized `name`/`username`/`directory`/`filename`); 🟡 H304 cap-gate family on privileged FS ops; 🟡 H305 fail-open `except Exception`; 🟡 H306 H7 Apache-2.0 strays (3/4); 💭 H307 no tests + lazy imports. **Fix order**: validate path params → cap-gate → surface fail-open → GPL headers → `test_var.py`.
